Functional reverse engineering · runtime observation · 2026-10-26

Every action,
observed

The interaction layer of the same three kitchen sites: menus, search, sliders, accordions, forms, modals, chat, catalogue downloads, language switching, storage and third-party calls. Each flow was executed in a real Chromium session; screenshots and step logs are retained. Form writes were blocked and recorded, never sent.
